## Narration 195

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A264)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 1</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُسَدَّدٌ، حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى، عَنْ شُعْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنِي الْحَكَمُ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الْحَمِيدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ مِقْسَمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي الَّذِي يَأْتِي امْرَأَتَهُ وَهِيَ حَائِضٌ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ يَتَصَدَّقُ بِدِينَارٍ أَوْ نِصْفِ دِينَارٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو دَاوُدَ هَكَذَا الرِّوَايَةُ الصَّحِيحَةُ قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ دِينَارٌ أَوْ نِصْفُ دِينَارٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَرُبَّمَا لَمْ يَرْفَعْهُ شُعْبَةُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 264]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/264" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated Abdullah ibn Abbas: The Prophet (ﷺ) said about a person who had intercourse with his wife while she was menstruating: He must give one dinar or half a dinar in alms. Abu Dawud said: The correct version says si: One dinar or half a dinar. Shu&#x27;bah (a narrator) did not sometimes narrate this tradition as a statement of the Prophet (ﷺ)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 264]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/264" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सैय्यदना इब्न अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने नबी करीम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से उस शख़्स के बारे में जो अपनी बीवी से हालत-ए-हैज़ में मुजामत करता है रिवायत किया है कि आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: एक दीनार सदक़ा करे या आधा दीनार। इमाम अबू दाऊद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि सहीह रिवायत ऐसे ही है कि एक दीनार या आधा दीनार, लेकिन शुअबाह इस रिवायत को बाज़ औक़ात मरफ़ूअ बयान न करते थे (बल्कि सैय्यदना इब्न अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) पर मौक़ूफ़ कर देते थे)।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 264]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/264" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سیدنا ابن عباس ؓ نے نبی کریم ﷺ سے اس شخص کے بارے میں جو اپنی بیوی سے حالت حیض میں مجامعت کرتا ہے روایت کیا ہے کہ آپ ﷺ نے فرمایا ”ایک دینار صدقہ کرے یا آدھا دینار۔“ امام ابوداؤد ؓ کہتے ہیں کہ صحیح روایت ایسے ہی ہے کہ ”ایک دینار یا آدھا دینار۔“ لیکن شعبہ اس روایت کو بعض اوقات مرفوع بیان نہ کرتے تھے۔ (بلکہ سیدنا ابن عباس ؓ پر موقوف کر دیتے تھے)۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 264]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Sayyidna Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne Nabi Kareem (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se us shakhs ke bare mein jo apni biwi se halat-e-haiz mein mujamat karta hai riwayat kiya hai ke Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ek dinar sadqa kare ya aadha dinar. Imam Abu Dawood (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ke sahih riwayat aise hi hai ke ek dinar ya aadha dinar, lekin Shu&#x27;bah is riwayat ko baaz auqat marfu&#x27; bayan na karte the (balkay Sayyidna Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) par mauquf kar dete the).</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 264]</p>

## Narration 196

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A266)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 1</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ الصَّبَّاحِ الْبَزَّازُ، حَدَّثَنَا شَرِيكٌ، عَنْ خُصَيْفٍ، عَنْ مِقْسَمٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ إِذَا وَقَعَ الرَّجُلُ بِأَهْلِهِ وَهِيَ حَائِضٌ فَلْيَتَصَدَّقْ بِنِصْفِ دِينَارٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو دَاوُدَ وَكَذَا قَالَ عَلِيُّ بْنُ بَذِيمَةَ عَنْ مِقْسَمٍ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم مُرْسَلاً وَرَوَى الأَوْزَاعِيُّ عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ أَبِي مَالِكٍ عَنْ عَبْدِ الْحَمِيدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ آمُرُهُ أَنْ يَتَصَدَّقَ بِخُمْسَىْ دِينَارٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَهَذَا مُعْضَلٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 266]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/266" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 266</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   266 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn ‘Abbas reported the Prophet (May peace be upon him) as saying; when a man has intercourse with his wife while she is menstruating, he must give half a dinar in alms. Abu Dawud said; ‘Ali b. Budhaimah reported similarly on the authority of Miqsam from the Prophet (May peace be upon him). Al-Awza’I narrated from Yazid b. Abi Malik, from ‘Abd al-Hamid b. ‘Abd al-Rahman from the Prophet (May peace be upon him); He ordered him to give two fifth of a dinar in alms. But this is a chain where two narrators (Miqsam and Ibn ‘Abbas) are missing</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 266]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/266" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 266</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   266 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जब आदमी अपनी बीवी से हालत-ए-हैज़ में जमाअ करे तो आधा दीनार सदक़ा करे। अबू दाऊद कहते हैं: और इसी तरह अली बिन बाज़िमह ने मिक़सम से, उन्होंने नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से मुरसलन रिवायत की है, और औज़ाई ने यज़ीद इब्न अबी मालिक से, यज़ीद ने अब्दुल हमीद बिन अब्दुर रहमान से, अब्दुल हमीद ने नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से रिवायत की कि आप ने उन्हें दो खुम्स दीनार सदक़ा करने का हुक्म फ़रमाया, और ये रिवायत मोअज़ल है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 266]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/266" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: جب آدمی اپنی بیوی سے حالت حیض میں جماع کرے تو آدھا دینار صدقہ کرے ۔ ابوداؤد کہتے ہیں: اور اسی طرح علی بن بذیمہ نے مقسم سے، انہوں نے نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے مرسلاً روایت کی ہے، اور اوزاعی نے یزید ابن ابی مالک سے، یزید نے عبدالحمید بن عبدالرحمٰن سے، عبدالحمید نے نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے روایت کی کہ آپ نے انہیں دو خمس دینار صدقہ کرنے کا حکم فرمایا، اور یہ روایت معضل ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 266]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/266" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 266</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   266 ]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jab aadmi apni biwi se halat-e-haiz mein jama kare toh aadha dinar sadqa kare. Abu Dawood kehte hain: Aur isi tarah Ali bin Bazimah ne Miqsam se, unhone Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se mursalan riwayat ki hai, aur Auzai ne Yazeed ibn Abi Malik se, Yazeed ne Abdul Hameed bin Abdur Rahman se, Abdul Hameed ne Nabi Akram (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se riwayat ki ke Aap ne unhein do khums dinar sadqa karne ka hukm farmaya, aur ye riwayat mo&#x27;zal hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 266]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/266" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 197

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A267)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 1</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَزِيدُ بْنُ خَالِدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ مَوْهَبٍ الرَّمْلِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا اللَّيْثُ بْنُ سَعْدٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ حَبِيبٍ، مَوْلَى عُرْوَةَ عَنْ نُدْبَةَ، مَوْلاَةِ مَيْمُونَةَ عَنْ مَيْمُونَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ يُبَاشِرُ الْمَرْأَةَ مِنْ نِسَائِهِ وَهِيَ حَائِضٌ إِذَا كَانَ عَلَيْهَا إِزَارٌ إِلَى أَنْصَافِ الْفَخِذَيْنِ أَوِ الرُّكْبَتَيْنِ تَحْتَجِزُ بِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 267]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/267" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   267 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Maimunah said:The Prophet (ﷺ) would contact and embrace any of his wives while she was menstruating. She would wear the wrapper up to half the the thighs or cover her knees with it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 267]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/267" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्मुल मोमिनीन मैमूना (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) कहती हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) अपनी बीवियों से हैज़ की हालत में मुबशरत (इख़्तिलात ओ मासस) करते थे जब उनके ऊपर निस्फ़ रानों तक या दोनों घुटनों तक तहबन्द होता जिससे वो आद किये होतीं.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 267]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/267" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام المؤمنین میمونہ رضی اللہ عنہا کہتی ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اپنی بیویوں سے حیض کی حالت میں مباشرت ( اختلاط و مساس ) کرتے تھے جب ان کے اوپر نصف رانوں تک یا دونوں گھٹنوں تک تہبند ہوتا جس سے وہ آڑ کئے ہوتیں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 267]</p>

## Narration 198

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A268)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 1</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُسْلِمُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ مَنْصُورٍ، عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، عَنِ الأَسْوَدِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، قَالَتْ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَأْمُرُ إِحْدَانَا إِذَا كَانَتْ حَائِضًا أَنْ تَتَّزِرَ ثُمَّ يُضَاجِعُهَا زَوْجُهَا وَقَالَ مَرَّةً يُبَاشِرُهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 268]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 268</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   268 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Aishah said; When anyone amongst us (the wives of the Prophet) menstruated, the Messenger of Allah (May peace be upon him) asked her to tie a waist wrapper (over her body) and then husband lay with her, or he (Shu’bah) said:embraced her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 268]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 268</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   268 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्मुल मोमिनीन आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) कहती हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हम में से किसी को जब वह हैज़ा होती इज़ार (तेहबंद) बांधने का हुक्म देते, फिर उसका शौहर उसके साथ सोता, एक बार रावी ने &#x27;युदाजिउहा&#x27; के बजाय &#x27;युबाशिरुहा&#x27; के अल्फ़ाज़ नक़ल किये हैं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 268]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام المؤمنین عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا کہتی ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ہم میں سے کسی کو جب وہ حائضہ ہوتی ازار ( تہبند ) باندھنے کا حکم دیتے، پھر اس کا شوہر ۱؎ اس کے ساتھ سوتا، ایک بار راوی نے «يضاجعها» کے بجائے «يباشرها» کے الفاظ نقل کئے ہیں۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 268]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/268" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">Ummul Momineen Ayesha (Radi Allahu Anha) kehti hain ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) hum mein se kisi ko jab wo haiza hoti izar (tehband) bandhne ka hukm dete, phir uska shohar uske saath sota, ek baar rawi ne &#x27;yudaji&#x27;uha&#x27; ke bajaye &#x27;yubashiruha&#x27; ke alfaaz naqal kiye hain.</p>

## Narration 199

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A272)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 1</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُوسَى بْنُ إِسْمَاعِيلَ، حَدَّثَنَا حَمَّادٌ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ، عَنْ عِكْرِمَةَ، عَنْ بَعْضِ، أَزْوَاجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ إِذَا أَرَادَ مِنَ الْحَائِضِ شَيْئًا أَلْقَى عَلَى فَرْجِهَا ثَوْبًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 272]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/272" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 272</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   272 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Narrated One of the Wives of the Prophet: Ikrimah reported on the authority of one of the wives of the Prophet (ﷺ) saying: When the Prophet (ﷺ) wanted to do something (i.e. kissing, embracing) with (his) menstruating wife, he would put a garment on her private part</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 272]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/272" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इक्रमः बाज़ अज़वाज-इ-मुतह्हरात (रज़ियल्लाहुु आंहुण) से रिवायत करते हैं कि नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हैज़ा से जब कुछ करना चाहते तो उनकी शर्मगाह पर एक कपडा डाल देते थे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 272]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/272" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عکرمہ بعض ازواج مطہرات رضی اللہ عنہن سے روایت کرتے ہیں کہ نبی اکرم صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم حائضہ سے جب کچھ کرنا چاہتے تو ان کی شرمگاہ پر ایک کپڑا ڈال دیتے تھے۔</p>

## Narration 200

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-abudawud%3A273)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 1</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عُثْمَانُ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا جَرِيرٌ، عَنِ الشَّيْبَانِيِّ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الأَسْوَدِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، - رضى الله عنها - قَالَتْ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَأْمُرُنَا فِي فَوْحِ حَيْضِنَا أَنْ نَتَّزِرَ ثُمَّ يُبَاشِرُنَا وَأَيُّكُمْ يَمْلِكُ إِرْبَهُ كَمَا كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَمْلِكُ إِرْبَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 273]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/273" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 273</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   273 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Aishah said; The Messenger of Allah (May peace be upon him) would ask us in the beginning of our menstruation to tie the waist-wrapper. Then he would embrace us. And who amongst you can have as much control over his desire as the Messenger of Allah (May peace be upon him) had over his desire?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 273]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/273" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><strong>Sunan Abi Dawud · 273</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Abi Dawud   273 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">उम्मुल मोमिनीन ऐषा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) कहती हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हमारे हैज़ की शिद्दत में हमें इज़ार (तहबन्द) बांधने का हुकुम फरमाते थे फिर हम से मुबाशिरत करते थे ख्वाहिश पर काबू पा सकता है जैसा के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को अपनी ख्वाहिश पर क़ाबू था?</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 273]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/273" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ام المؤمنین عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا کہتی ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ہمارے حیض کی شدت میں ہمیں ازار ( تہبند ) باندھنے کا حکم فرماتے تھے پھر ہم سے مباشرت کرتے تھے، اور تم میں سے کون اپنی خواہش پر قابو پا سکتا ہے جیسا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو اپنی خواہش پر قابو تھا؟ ۱؎۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 273]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/abudawud/273" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Abi Dawud</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ummul Momineen Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) kehti hain ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) hamare haiz ki shiddat mein humein izaar (tehband) bandhne ka hukum farmate the phir hum se mubashirat karte the, aur tum mein se kaun apni khwahish par qaboo pa sakta hai jaisa ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko apni khwahish par qaboo tha?</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Abi Dawud · 273]</p>
